1.4 Details of section: Work Completed & Line Commissioned in Year 2021-22.
1.5 Objective/Remarks
1.5.1 Objective:
Jalgaon-Bhusawal Section is an over-saturated section of Central Railway. This section deals
with the traffic from four directions viz. Mumbai, Surat, Nagpur and Khandwa. At present, the
major operational constraint is Jalgaon Junction, which is dealing with traffic from Mumbai and
Surat directions. Traffic moving towards Surat (coming from Bhusawal from UP side) has to
cross DN main line at Jalgaon Yard and similarly traffic coming from Surat is required to be dealt
with traffic coming from Mumbai, Pune, Solapur and Aurangabad. Considering the present
volume of traffic and also the expected growth of traffic in view of proposed doubling of Surat-
Jalgaon line, faster movement of traffic is essential in Jalgaon-Bhusawal section. It is also
necessary to segregate the traffic in Jalgaon-Bhusawal section, in order to avoid unwarranted
detention of traffic in surface Crossing at Jalgaon Yard.
1.5.2 Remarks:
● The third line between Bhadli- Bhusawal Section (12.62 Km) of Central Railway, Bhusaval
Division was inspected by CRS on 5th Feb. 2019 and speed trial was done at 120 Kmph. The
Section has been opened for passenger traffic by CRS vide authorization letter no. C-12
(205)/2019 Dt. 07-02-19. Bhadli – Bhusawal commissioned on 07.02.2019.
● The third line between Jalgaon - Bhadli Section (11.51 Km) of Central Railway, Bhusaval
Division was inspected by CRS on 11.06.21 and speed trial was done at 120 Kmph. The
Section has been opened for passenger traffic by CRS vide authorization letter no.
12(250)/2021/367 dated: 11.06.21 and Section Commissioned on 22.08.2021. Speed
Restriction in BSL – Jalgaon 3rd. line section to 110 KMPH in May 2022.
● Revised Estimate submitted to Railway Board by HQ on 12-03-2025.
1.6 Issues with State Govt.: NIL
1.7 Issues with other Central Ministries (Forest and Wild life Clearance): Nil.
1.8 Year wise Planning for commissioning of complete project: Project Commissioned in
Year 2021-22.

3.5 Objective
Jalgaon-Bhusawal Section is an over-saturated section of Central Railway. This section deals
with the traffic from four directions viz. Mumbai, Surat, Nagpur and Khandwa. At present, the
major operational constraint is Jalgaon Junction, which is dealing with traffic from Mumbai and
Surat directions. Traffic moving towards Surat (coming from Bhusawal from UP side) has to
cross DN main line at Jalgaon Yard and similarly traffic coming from Surat is required to be dealt
with traffic coming from Mumbai, Pune, Solapur and Aurangabad. Considering the present
volume of traffic and also the expected growth of traffic in view of proposed doubling of Surat-
Jalgaon line, faster movement of traffic is essential in Jalgaon-Bhusawal section. It is also
necessary to segregate the traffic in Jalgaon-Bhusawal section, in order to avoid unwarranted
detention of traffic in surface crossing at Jalgaon Yard.

1.6 Objective & Remarks 3rd & 4th line:
1.6.1 Objective:
● For 3rd line:This is a convergent section of North South & East West route and identified high
density route. It is necessary to construct the 3rd line to eliminate bottle-neck.
● For 4th line: This section is one of the busiest and important sections of Railway. Apart from the
traffic of both the Trunk routes, many Private siding/PFT proposals are in the advance stage,
which will also contribute additional traffic on the section. Hence, for uninterrupted and speedy
movement of trains, construction of the 4th line on the section is necessary.
1.6.2. Remark’s 3rd& 4th line:
• Tender for all Civil engineering works i.e EW , bridges , Station building , Track work , Ballast
supply in Sewagram- Nagpur section awarded.
• Total Commissioned section Seloo Rd to Ajni 61.55km x 2
• Wardha- SelooRd Section : Earthwork & Minor bridges work is in progress in Sewagram- Seloo
Rd section. All 3 nos major& Important bridges in SEGM- SLOR section are completed.

2.5 Objective /Remarks: -
2.5.1 Objective-
This section serves passengers as well as goods traffic from North to South. The line capacity
utilization of the section is saturated and running additional mail/express and goods traffic over
the section causes detention to the trains. Also, it is an important section from goods originating
point of view as many collieries and many sidings are proposed on the section. For smooth
running of anticipated traffic, the 3rd line between Wardha – Ballarshah is essential.
2.6 Issues with State Govt.:
• Land acquisition is involved in 132 Km length for a total 129.93 Ha. All Proposals submittedto
Collector Wardha & Chandrapur. JMR of 43 villages completed. Advance payment of ₹ 91.76 Cr.
made. Land acquisition is to be expedited by the State Govt. Early execution of the sale deed is
required.
• Meetings at District Collectors & SDO’s are being regularly conducted by the Railway field
officers for early acquisition of land.
2.7 Issues with other Central Ministries (Forest and Wild life Clearance):
2.8 Forest Clearance: (0.29 Ha). Pending with U.A. Proposal yet to be submitted Online.

3.6 Issues with State Govt.:
Forest Land – Land has been acquired.
Issues with State Govt.:
Acquisition of land in Satara district:
Two writ petition no. 13514: dtd. 13.07.2021 & 19549 dtd. 12.10.2021 have been filed in Hon’ble
High Court.
First petition by the YogeshJaganathChavan& second petition by Ramachandra Sampat Mane &
Others V/S Union of India.
Writ petition no. 13514
Petition pertains to payment of compensation for land acquired in 1968 at time of gauge
conversion.
Hearings took place on 11.08.2021, 09.09.21, 14.09.21, 28.09.21.
In last hearing on 23.11.21, court directed District Collector Satara to resolve the issue in 12 weeks
and asked petitioner to first exhaust all other legal channels available before coming to high court.
Collector Satara has held meeting twice with Farmers.
He has now completed hearings with Farmers & Railway and has given final orders on 06.04.22
that farmers to come through proper channel and approach concerned SDO first. SDO hearing held
on 27.07.2022.
Work completed in this section by forceful possession.
Writ petition no. 19549
Petition pertains to payment of compensation for land acquired in 1941 at time of construction of
new MG line.
Last hearing took place on 17.11.2021.
High Court asked Railway and State authorities to file their detailed reply in 15 days time which
was done in time. However, hearing scheduled on 03.01.22, could not take place due to change of
Judges in the Bench. On 06-04-22, High Court has directed state authorities to carry out the joint
24

--- Page 30 ---
measurement and resolve by 29th Jun’22. JMR done on 04.06.22 further process is in progress at
SDO/KRD office. Hearing scheduled on 27.03.2023.
Work completed in this section by forceful possession.
The landowners/Farmers were not allowing to execute earthwork & Bridge work in Railway
land, resulting in holding up of the doubling work in Satara District. State authorities are being
regularly chased for early land acquisition at various levels.
Further, Regular meetings are being conducted with concerned Collectors, SDOs & DFO to
sort out the land acquisition issues coming in Pune, Satara & Sangli Dist. Last meeting was
conducted on 11.12.23, 12.12.23, 17.12.2023 & 18.12.2023 various levels.

4.5 Objective:
● Manmad-Jalgaon is one of the busiest and important sections of Railway. Apart from the traffic of
both the trunk routes, many private sidings and PFT proposals are in advance, which will also
contribute additional traffic on the section. Hence, for uninterrupted and speedy movement of
trains, construction of the 3rd line on the section is necessary.
4.6 Issues with State Govt.:
● Total land required is 16.4565 Ha out of which 16.4055 Ha land is acquired. Land acquisition is
required to be expedited. Now,this project has been declared as Special Railway Project under
Railway (Amendment) Act, 2008 and balance land acquisition is being carried out under the
provision of this Act.
● Meetings at District Collectors & SDO’s are being regularly conducted by the Railway field officers
for early acquisition of land.

1.5 Objective / Remarks
1.5.1 Objective:
The capacity of Itarsi–Nagpur section is restricted to 52 trains because of Ghat section. The line
capacity utilization of the section in 2013-14 was 162.9% in Amla - Itarsi section and 133% in
Nagpur - Amla section. After construction of 3rd line, it will be possible to run additional
mail/express and goods trains in Itarsi –Nagpur section. It will also be helpful for smooth &
speedy movement of trains.

3.5 Objective & Remarks:
3.5.1 Objective:
Kalyan-Kasara (67.62 Km) is an electrified double line section of Mumbai Division of Central
Railway. This corridor is known as the North-East and is a part of CSTM-HWH Group ‘A’ route.
It is divided into 3 sub-sections viz., Kalyan-Titwala (10.84 Kms), working on Automatic Block
Signaling, Titwala-Asangaon (21.38 Kms) and Asangaon-Kasara (35.4 Kms) both working on
Absolute Block System. All the three streams of traffic viz. Suburban, Mail/Express and freight
trains use the same pair of lines between Kalyan & Kasara. The section is at present one of the
busiest and important sections of Central Railway. Construction of a bi-directional 3rd line for
Mail/Express/Freight services on the section will improve line capacity of the section and
provide relief to saturated sections.
3.5.2 Remarks:
● All 11 No. ESPs are approved. After approval, 4th line sanctioned between KYN-ASO hence
6 ESPs again revised.
● Construction of Major Bridges in progress.
● Contract for the affected structure in KYN-KSRA section has been awarded and 75% work
completed and balance 25% is in progress. One tender for balance structures has been
awarded. Work is in progress.
● Track laying/Linking Contract awarded on 09.08.17. It was terminated on account of
Agency.New track laying/linking contract awarded on 06.11.2023.
● Tender for ballast supply in Titwala-Asangaon finalized & LOA issued on 27.05.2019. Work
started & supply being taken in the yards & level crossings where space is available. Supply
of 11300 cum taken till date.
● S&T outdoor works & Telecom work tenders awarded. Indoor tender under preparation.
● Electrical work of OHE infringement removal in progress.

1.5 Remarks/ Objective:
1.5.1 Objective:
● To boost socio-economic growth of the undeveloped Marathwada region of Maharashtra and to
provide improved connectivity, this new line project of 261.25 km was sanctioned. It aims to
connect two existing rail head stations of Ahmednagar (ANG) of Central Railway (CR) and Parli
Vaijnath (PV) of South-Central railway (SCR). In January 2010, Government of Maharashtra
conveyed the approval of cost sharing on 50:50 basis.
● The alignment traversing through Ahmednagar – Narayndoh- Loni- Solapurwadi- Dhanora- Kada
– Ashti – Kinhi-Bavi- Amalner- jatnandur- Eganwadi – Raimoh - Rajuri– Beed – Wadwani – Sirsala
– Tokwadi- Parli Vaijnath lies in Ahmednagar and Beed districts.
61

--- Page 67 ---
1.5.2 Remarks:
● The work between Ashti to Parli Vaijnath is in progress and hindrances due to Land acquisition.
● The project has been declared as a “Special Railway Project” vide Govt. of India Gazette
Notification No 1342 dated 02.06.2016. Regular meetings at District, Commissioner and State
level are being held.

5.5 Remarks & Objective
5.5.1 Objective:
● The proposed alignment from Vaibhavwadi to Kolhapur passes through the districts of
Kolhapur, Ratnagiri and Sindhudurg of Maharashtra state. The proposed alignment would link
Kolhapur railway station on the existing Pune-Kolhapur section of Central Railway with
Vaibhavwadi railway station on the existing Roha-Madgaon section of Konkan Railway.
● The proposed link would reduce the distances for thermal coal transportation for a number of
power plants in Maharashtra & Karnataka.
● The lack of Rail connectivity between Central & Konkan regions leads to longer supply routes
for power plants in the Northern Karnataka & Southern Maharashtra region. This leads to coal
supply coming from ports like Mumbai, JNPT, Krishnapatnam& New Mangalore are taken via the
longer rail route of RN-PANVEL-PUNE route. The addition of Vaibhavwadi-Kolhapur is expected
to directly benefit the districts of Belgaum, Hubli, Hospet, Bellary, Bijapur, Gulbarga, Latur, Parli
& Solapur and create a shorter evacuation route to Western ports.
● There will also be reduction in distance and time taken for freight trains running on the existing
Vaibhavwadi-Roha-Pune route and Miraj-Pune-Panvel-Pen route if the same is routed via the
Vaibhavwadi–Kolhapur project line.
75

--- Page 81 ---

● Modified DPR sent to Board on 07.05.2017 with estimated cost of ₹ 3438.51 Cr. ROR (+) 1.72%.
DPR has been sanctioned by Railway Board vide their letter No.2017/W-I/CR /DPR/VBW-KOP,
Dt. 19.08.19. Proposal for appointing M/s RITES as consultant was under process.
● Vide Board's letter No. 2019/W-1/CR./NL/VBW-KOP dt 25.09.19 that no expenditure should
be done for the project for which sanction was conveyed vide Railway Board's letter
dt.19.08.19 & 09.09.2019. Remarks have been submitted to the Board on 03.01.2020.
● Final communication regarding authorization of expenditure is awaited.
● Alignment has been validated and updated DPR has been sent to Rly. Board on 31st May
2023.Special limited tender has been awarded to konkan railway for updating of old DPR.
● Upgradation of feasibility/DPR work in progress by KRCL.
5.6 Status conveyed by Railway Board on 06.12.2021:
● Approved by CCEA on 06.08.2019. However, PMO vide letter dated 08.08.2019 forwarded the
comments of DoE (MoF) and directed to re-examine the estimate in light of comments of DoE
(MoF).
● Subsequently, PMO vide letter dated 11.09.2019 and 18.09.2019 advised that earlier CCEA
approval should not be taken as authorization for expenditure.
● Para wise reply on DoE (MoF) observations given to PMO vide Railway Board letter dated
28.10.2020. In railway’s letter, detailed remarks on observations of DoE on estimate were given
and it was explained that estimate remains same even after examining and complying the DoE’s
observations and permission for expenditure was sought.
● Again, request for authorization to take up the work sent to PMO vide letter dated 31.08.2021.
● Subsequently, in Nov’2021 e-mail regarding holding a meeting regarding clarification on
estimate was received on 12.11.2021. Meeting was held on 16.11.2021 and issues/observations
were clarified to DoE officials. Second meeting was also held on 25.11.2021.
● The PMO vide letter dated 10.01.2022 has again enclosed further comments of Dept. of
Expenditure MOF. Central Railway replied on 09.05.2022. Decision awaited.

13.5 Objective/Remarks: -
13.5.1 Objective:
This new rail line project has been sanctioned to provide improved connectivity between
Khandesh and Northern Region of Maharashtra and Satpura and Narmada Valley Region of
Madhya Pradesh. It will connect Manmad (MMR) of Central Railway (CR) and Mhow (Which is
recently named as Dr. Ambedkar Nagar) of Western Railway (WR). New rail link will open a
direct route from Mumbai (via Manmad) to Indore. Both cities have economic and historical
importance which will contribute significantly to the socio-economic development of backward
regions of Maharashtra and Madhya Pradesh State.
90

--- Page 96 ---
13.5.2 Remarks:
• DPR with detailed estimate amounting ₹ 8857.98 Cr. sent to Board on 14.07.2017 with ROR (+)
3.336%. However, as per Railway Board's letter No. 2018/Infra/12/I dated. 31.08.2018 issued
by ED/Traffic/PPP, the above work Indore-Manmad New B.G. Railway Line has been
entrusted to execute by IPRCL/Mumbai through a transparent Expression of Interest (EOI).
• Previously, the project has been considered by Infrastructure Directorate of railway Board for
taking up it under Joint Venture (JV) model. Hence, a Memorandum of understanding (MoU)
has been entered into between Jawaharlal Nehru Port Trust (JNPT) and State Governments of
Madhya Pradesh and Maharashtra to set up a JV company for taking up this project with the
Indian Port Railway Corporation Limited (IPRCL) as the executing agency. Subsequently, JNPT
has shown its unwillingness to continue with this project keeping in view of the plans to invest
in other projects. Therefore, Railway Board’s vide letter no.2017/W-I/CR/DPR/Indore-
Manmad dtd. 25.07.2022, advised that the project has been transferred to Gati Shakti
directorate to deal with as a Railway Project.
• Further Railway Board advised that in case, FLS needs to be carried out again the Railway
should mention the same clearly with detailed justification along with cost of FLS & process
case to Railway Board for further processing. FLS tender invited. Estimate for Part alignment
(38Km) FLS at the cost of Rs 95 Lakhs sanctioned on 12.10.22 by GM. The DPR to be updated as
per Railway Board instruction and required to be resubmitted to Rly Board. Consultant
finalized & LOA issued on 04.01.2023 to M/s Monarch Surveyors and Consultants Ltd Pune.
Traffic survey, Alignment Survey & Geotech survey work completed. DPR submitted to HQ on
18.07.2023.HQ finance observations obtained on 20.07.2023 and compliances of HQ finance
submitted on 21.07.2023. DPR after corrections submitted to HQ and finance vetting obtained
on 31.07.2023. Project Evaluation committee of Railway Board reviewed MMR-Indore NL
project on 01.09.2023 and observation on 04.09.2023. Compliance to observation in progress.
• State Government of Maharashtra and State Governments of Madhya Pradesh has been
requested for 50 % Cost sharing falling in respective states respectively. ED/GS/RB has again
reviewed MMR-INDB project on 29.09.2023. Project Evaluation Committee Re-reviewed the
project on 27.10.2023. Revised Detailed Estimate submitted to HQ finance. HQ finance vetting
obtained on 10.11.2023 for civil portion. PEC again reviewed the project on 20.11.20223 with
revised EIRR & FIRR after considering the traffic projection of proposed Nashik- Pune Project.
Final DPR duly compliances of PED meeting observations submitted to railway board on
28.12.2023. NPG meeting done 27.02.2024 and EBR done on 15.04.2024.Updated DPR of this
Project sanctioned on 19.09.2024.Project has been declared as special Railway project" on
26.09.24. CALA has been appointed by District collectors of Khargone, Dhar, Badwani, Dhule,

Narratives:-
Total Scope of the Project:
The work comprises of providing double line from Nerul to Uran, Kile Junction to Belapur and Diversion of
track between Nerul- Belapur and modification in Uran & Belapur yard. Major civil works involved in this
project are as under:-
i) Laying and linking of double line BG track from
a) Nerul to Uran yard (2 x 25.00 km)
b) Kile Junction to Belapur (2 x 2.5 km)
ii) Providing earth work in embankment from Nerul to Uran except for cutting between ch. 8000 to
9000
iii) Construction of an ‘Important’ bridge across Panvel Creek for a span of 751 m. with navigation
clearances (Completed).
iv) Construction of a tunnel for a length of 100m(Completed)
v) Construction of 5 major bridges. (3 completed and 2 work in progress)
vi) Construction of a flyover bridge near Jasai Junction to provide grade separated crossing over
Panvel-JNPT Railway line. (Work in progress.)
vii) Construction of Viaduct from CH. 1100 to CH. 1700 (Completed).
viii) Construction of 67 minor bridges/Service crossings (56 Completed) and extension of 18 existing
bridges.(Completed)
ix) Construction of road over bridges – 5 nos. (2 completed and 2 work in progress) and road under
bridges – 14 nos. 12 Completed)
x) Construction of station buildings – 10 stations, viz. Seawood, Kille, Targhar, Bamandongri,
Kharkopar, Ghavan, Ranjanpada, Nava Sheva, Dronagiri, and Uran including S.M. office, booking
offices, relay room, panel room, battery room, power room and other offices.
xi) Construction of 2 nos. commuter subways/FOBs at each station.
xii) Construction of residential quarters – 564 nos. for all departments.
xiii) Expansion of carshed at Sanpada for AOH.
xiv) Provision of stabling siding – 9 nos.
xv) Construction of 2 nos. 220/25 KV single unit traction station
xvi) Construction of 1 No. sectioning post (SP) and 2 nos. sub-sectioning posts (2) for OHE.
xvii) Construction of two numbers new telephone exchange buildings, 1 no. UHF station.
xviii) Construction of offices/depots for Engg., S&T and Electrical supervisors.
xix) Construction of tower wagon shed for OHE at URAN, running rooms at Seawood and Uran
xx) Goods platform with COP at Uran
Land Acquisition (Section & Location wise: Total scope, progress, status, expected date of
completion): 188 ha. Land acquisition acquired by CIDCO.
Resettlement & Rehabilitation: (Section & Location wise: Total scope, progress, status, expected
date of completion):- NIL
Clearances/Approvals required from other Depts./Organisations/Ministry & its Status: NIL
Cumulative achievements/progress: Phase –I (Nerul/Belapur –Kharkopar): CRS Inspection has
been carried out on 30.10.2018, and commissioned on 11 Nov'2018.
Achievements/progress during the month: PH-II (Kharkopar to Uran): Work completed &
commissioned on 14.03.2023 & Project completed.

Narratives:-
Objective:
• The work of proposing additional pairs of lines between CSTM and Kurla was felt necessary to segregate
long distance traffic from the suburban traffic as well as to improve the punctuality of suburban services
on Central Railway. This additional pair of lines is being connected with the 5th & 6th lines of Kurla-
Thane section.
• As of now 5th & 6th lines b/w CSMT-Kurla- under construction by CR Kurla-Thane- exists Thane-Diva-
constructed by MRVC in Feb 2022.
108

--- Page 114 ---
• The project has 2 Phase. 1st Phase from Km 18.00 (VVH) to parel Km (7.292) 2nd Phase from Parel
(extension) to CSMT 1st Phase work is in progress.
• Harbour line is proposed as elevated flyover to eliminate the surface crossing of good lines to improve the
services at Kurla.
• The proposed 5th & 6th lines between CSTM and Kurla will take off from Km 0.150 at CSTM and shall pass
through Masjid, Sandhurst Road, Byculla, Chinchpokli, Currey Road, Parel, Dadar, Matunga, Sion and
Kurla Stations and will join to the KYN end of Kurla Station at Km. 17.435.
• M/S RITES engaged as consultants on 25.06.2009.
Total Scope of the Project:
• Construction of 5th & 6th line from CSTM to Kurla of total length 17.4Km. through stations i.e., Masjid,
Sandhurst Road, Byculla, Chinchlpokli, Curry Road, Parel, Dadar, Matunga, Sion and Kurla.
• Construction of Parel Suburban Terminus.
• Construction of Kurla Flyover.
• Re-Construction of ROB at Sion and Dharavi
• Reconstruction of King Circle Rail Flyover between Matunga & Sion.
Land Acquisition:
• For phase–I:- Project (Kurla to Parel), total 5 proposals for 10139.24 sqm area have been submitted.
(Govt. Land- 3639.34 sqm, Pvt. Land- 6499.90 sqm, Land Acquired = 5748.23 Sqm., Balance = 4391.01
Sqm.)
• 3 proposals have been submitted to Collector/Mumbai (total area 5670.34 sqm) Land transfer is held up
due to shifting of PAPs not done by MMRDA so far.
• 2 proposals pertain to Collector/Bandra Suburban (total area 4390.65 sqm) in which necessary
documents have been submitted. . Letter written to Collector Mumbai for acquiring land under section
10(D) of Act 2018.
• Letter replied awaiting action by Sub. Divisional Officer (Land) VVH for acquiring land under 10 (D) of
RRLHPPB act 2018.
• Land acquisition process is in advance stage.
• For phase-II:- From Parel to CSTM, acquisition of about 6767 Sqm of Land (1743 Sqm at Byculla + 5024
Sqm at Masjid) and 8200 Sqm of private structures & buildings between Sandhurst Road and CSTM is
involved which needs to be relocated first before dismantling. Land identification & collection of land
records are in progress.
• Private structures & buildings include 4 Nos. (G+6), 1 No. (G+5), 2Nos. (G+4), 2 Nos (G+3), 1 No. (G+2), 1
No. (G+1).
• In order to avoid this cumbersome process, Central Railway had proposed alternate alignment after
taking concurrence from the State government.
• In principal approval’ of State Govt. for alternate alignment is yet to be received.
• The issue of alternate alignment is also the part of CSMT Station Development Project, being taken up by
IRSDC in the High-Level Committee chaired by Chief Secretary of Maharashtra.
• It has been appraised in the HLC meeting in June 2021, chaired by Chief Secretary, of Maharashtra to do
the comprehensive mobility planning over PD Mello Road, accordingly, utility mapping over & under the
road has been done.
• Meeting with all the stakeholders like MbPT, MCGGM, MMRDA, Traffic dept. have been done in Feb 2022,
March 2022, April 2022, May 2022 and further in progress.
• Joint site visit of Rly & MbPT officials held on 30.03.2022.

through port area was discussed over which Chairman MbPT principally agreed and suggested that utility
corridor to be properly planned in consultation with all stake holders.

feasible.

member identification work is in progress.

R & R
● R&R of balance 634 PAHs (R&R for 41 PAHs already done in 2017 out of 675) has to be doneby MMRDA
for which Baseline Socio-Economic Survey of affected structures/households has been completed in
Dec. 2020 by SPARC (Society for the Promotion of Area Resource Centre’s,an NGO) nominated by MRVC
and submitted its report to MRVC/Railway in Jan. 2021.
• Railway submitted this report to MMRDA in Feb.2021.
● Joint verification of 634 Project affected structures/ households has been done by MMRDA, Central
Railway and MRVC in March 2021.Rehabilitation scheme submitted by MMRDA in Aug.2021.
● Survey report of 60 nos .PAPs present on W.R land at Dadar received from W .R and submitted to
MMRDA in Sep. 2021 for further action, joint verification of which has been done on04.02.2022.
● Survey report of 23 nos. leftover PAPs (8 nos at PR & 15 nos at Kings Circle) has been submitted to
MMRDA on 26.11.2021, joint verification of which has been done 04.02.2022.
● During online PMG meeting on 13.04.2022 as well as meeting with Chief Secretary, Maharashtra on
13.04.2022, MMRDA Commissioner assured R&R at the earliest.
● R&R Work was is held up due to non-completion of units for PAPs by MMRDA. As per MMRDA, the
shifting of PAPs was be completed by NOV-2024.
● As per MMRDA, shifting of PAPs will be done after Maharashtra state Election scheduled in November
2024.
● PAPs allotment started in Jan-25. Out of 714, 656 allotment done so far in which 528 PAPs has been
given key possession & for others, R&R is in progress by MMRDA.

Army FOB, two stabling lines and RRI building have been commissioned.
• Construction of Central FOB at Sion commissioned on 03.03.2019 and KYN end FOB on 20.02.2022.
